Expand description

A Pantone Matching System (PMS) color name.

The enum covers 1342 coated (C) and 1335 uncoated (U) spot colors of the Pantone system - named process colors (Pantone Process Blue C), named mixes (Pantone Cool Gray 1 U) and the numbered colors (Pantone 485 C), whose variants carry a Pms prefix since Rust identifiers must not start with a digit. The suffix letter is a PantoneSuffix.

Names parse case-insensitively via FromStr; Display and the wire format use the canonical Pantone <name> <suffix> form.

The stored sRGB values are common approximations of the print colors, e.g. as published on brandible.de (coated) and in the Pantone_finder dataset (uncoated) - Pantone colors are defined for print, so the values are indicative rather than authoritative. The CMYK builds of Self::to_cmyk follow the Pantone-CMYK-RGB-Hex dataset. PANTONE is a trademark of Pantone LLC.

Implementations§

Source§

impl PantoneNamedColor

Source

pub const ALL: &'static [Self]

All Pantone colors, sorted by canonical name.

Source

pub fn suffix(&self) -> PantoneSuffix

The fan-book variant encoded in the name’s suffix letter.

Source

pub fn counterpart(&self) -> Option<Self>

The same ink on the other substrate - C becomes U and vice versa - if the catalog contains the counterpart. Colors without a coated/uncoated pair (e.g. M variants or catalog gaps) yield None.

Source

pub const fn as_str(&self) -> &'static str

The canonical Pantone <name> <suffix> string.

Source

pub const fn to_color(&self) -> Color<u8>

The color as opaque byte components.

Source

pub fn nearest(color: &Color<u8>) -> Self

The nearest Pantone color to color in the sRGB space - squared Euclidean distance over the RGB channels, alpha ignored.

Source

pub const fn to_cmyk(&self) -> Option<Cmyk>

The published CMYK build of the color, if the reference data covers it.

The values are community reference values for the coated guide - metallic and newer colors have no published build and return None, as do all U variants (uncoated CMYK builds differ and are not covered by the dataset). For a naive conversion of any color see Cmyk::from(Color<u8>).
